# GasGas MC 250 2023: aftermarket parts

4757 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 346 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 690 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3721 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
